287:def gcd_recursion(sign_a, sign_b, r, s, t):\n    r_1, r_2 = r[0], r[1]\n    s_1, s_2 = s[0], s[1]\n    t_1, t_2 = t[0], t[1]\n    if r_2 == 0:\n        return [s_1 * sign_a, t_1 * sign_b]\n    else:\n        quotient = r_1 // r_2\n        r_ = [r_2, r_1 - quotient * r_2]\n        s_ = [s_2, s_1 - quotient * s_2]\n        t_ = [t_2, t_1 - quotient * t_2]\n        return gcd_recursion(sign_a, sign_b, r_, s_, t_)

294:def bb_intersection_over_union(boxA, boxB):\n    xA = max(boxA[0], boxB[0])\n    yA = max(boxA[1], boxB[1])\n    xB = min(boxA[2], boxB[2])\n    yB = min(boxA[3], boxB[3])\n    inter_area = max(0, (xB - xA) + 1)\n    boxA_area = (boxA[2] - boxA[0]) + 1\n    boxB_area = (boxB[3] - boxB[1]) + 1\n    iou = inter_area / (boxA_area + boxB_area - inter_area)\n    return iou\n

804:def poly_decay(epoch):\n    global_step = max(2 ** (epoch - 1), 1)\n    power = 0.5\n    lr_init = 0.001\n    lr_end = 0.0001\n    decay_steps = 10\n    if epoch <= 10:\n        return lr_init\n    elif epoch >= 50:\n        return lr_end\n    elif epoch > 50 + decay_steps:\n        return lr_end * (power ** (epoch - (50 + decay_steps)))\n    else:\n        return (lr_init - lr_end) * ((1 - power * ((epoch - 50) / decay_steps)) ** lr_init)\n

972:def iou(bbox1, bbox2):\n    x1 = max(bbox1[0], bbox2[0])\n    y1 = max(bbox1[1], bbox2[1])\n    x2 = min(bbox1[2], bbox2[2])\n    y2 = min(bbox1[3], bbox2[3])\n    i = max(0, x2 - x1)\n    j = max(0, y2 - y1)\n    r = i * j\n    a1 = (bbox1[2] - bbox1[0] - 1) * (bbox1[3] - bbox1[1] - 1)\n    a2 = (bbox2[2] - bbox2[0] - 1) * (bbox2[3] - bbox2[1] - 1)\n    a = a1 + a2\n    return r / a\n

1953:def correct_box(box, z):\n    def max_func(x, y):\n        return y if x < y else x\n    def min_func(x, y):\n        return x if x < y else y\n    x0 = box[0]\n    y0 = box[1]\n    x1 = box[2]\n    y1 = box[3]\n    new_x0 = max_func(0, min_func(x0, x1))\n    new_x1 = min_func((2 ** z) - 1, max_func(x0, x1))\n    new_y0 = max_func(0, min_func(y0, y1))\n    new_y1 = min_func((2 ** z) - 1, max_func(y0, y1))\n    return [new_x0, new_y0, new_x1, new_y1]\n

1965:def struct_size(items):\n    member_sum = 0\n    pad = 0\n    most_aligned = 1\n    for item in items:\n        elem = item[0]\n        align = item[1]\n        most_aligned = max(most_aligned, align)\n        pad = (most_aligned - member_sum) % most_aligned\n        member_sum += pad + elem\n        pad = (most_aligned - member_sum) % most_aligned\n        member_sum += pad\n    return [member_sum, most_aligned]\n

2258:def ring_edges(n, triples):\n    def triple_edges(a, b, k):\n        if a == b and 2 * k == n:\n            limit = k\n        else:\n            limit = n\n        offset = a * n + b\n        edges = []\n        for i in range(limit):\n            edge = [\n                (i + offset) % n,\n                (k + i + offset) % n\n            ]\n            edges.append(edge)\n        return edges\n    result = []\n    for triple in triples:\n        a, b, k = triple\n        result.extend(triple_edges(a, b, k))\n    return result\n
